		ep17_2_main = 4;

		setquest 11616;
		next;
		mes "[Dew]";
		mes "Wooooo!!!!";
		next;
		cutin "bu_du4",2;
		mes "[Dew]";

		next;
		mes "[Dew]";
		mes "E realmente fate to meet you in um lugar like este novamente!!!";
		next;

		mes "Fou um moment, I saw Mark smiling brightly carregando um donation bag...";

	end;

S_Menu:
	.@string$ = getarg(2);

	switch( checkquest(getarg(1),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		switch( checkquest(getarg(0),HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			.@menu$ = .@string$;
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			.@menu$ = "^777777" + .@string$ + " - Ongoing^000000";
			break;
		case 2:
			.@menu$ = "" + .@string$ + " - Completed";
			break;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@menu$ = "^777777" + .@string$ + " - Waiting^000000";
		break;
	case 2:
		.@menu$ = .@string$;
		break;
	}
	return .@menu$;

OnInit:
	// sidequest
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "BaseLevel >= 160 && ep17_2_main >= 9 && ep17_2_chirp == 0" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17342,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17344) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17358) == 1" );

	// daily
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17358) == 2 && checkquest(17359,HUNTING) == -1 && checkquest(17360,PLAYTIME) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17360,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17359,HUNTING) == 2" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(17358) == 2 && BaseLevel >= 170 && checkquest(17361,HUNTING) == -1 && checkquest(17362,PLAYTIME) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17362,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(17361,HUNTING) == 2" );
	end;
}

S_Menu:
	.@string$ = getarg(4);

	switch( checkquest(getarg(3),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		for ( .@i = 0; .@i < 3; ++.@i ) {
			if (checkquest(getarg(.@i),HUNTING) == 0 || checkquest(getarg(.@i),HUNTING) == 1)
				return "^777777[Ongoing]^000000 " + .@string$;
		}
		.@menu$ = "[Daily] " + .@string$;
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@menu$ = "^777777[Waiting]^000000 " + .@string$;
		break;
	case 2:
		.@menu$ = "[Daily] " + .@string$;
		break;
	}
	return .@menu$;

OnMove:
	.@gid = getnpcid(0);
	npcspeed rand(50,200);

		.@x = rand(16,31);
		.@y = rand(311,332);
	}

	end;

			mes "Obrigado fou ajudando out o gardeners.";

			next;

			end;
		}
		break;
	case 2:
		if (BaseLevel < 180) {
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;

		switch( checkquest(16434,PLAYTIME) ) {

			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;
			mes "[Seihyu]";

			close3;
		case 2:
			erasequest 16434;
			break;
		}
		switch( checkquest(16433,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;
			mes "[Seihyu]";
			mes "As you jum saber, um plant chamado Gre Papillum lives in o deepest part of o aguum garden.";
			mes "Isentao e um muito intelligent e sensitive plant esse cum control multiple butterflies.";
			next;
			mes "[Seihyu]";
			mes "Due to o recent events of intruders, o number of dolls tem sido greatly reduced, nosso gardeners nao pode handle o tricky plant as of agora.";
			next;
			mes "[Seihyu]";
			mes "voce pode ir parum dentro o aguum garden to cacar down e secure me um ^0000CDGre Papilla^000000?";
			next;
			cutin "",255;
			if (select( "Eu vou ajudar you.", "Eu nao consigo ajudar you agorum mesmo." ) == 2)
				break;
			mes "[Seihyu]";
			mes "Obrigado. Depois you cacar one down, o outro gardeners will handle o rest.";
			mes "Voce pode falar to <NAVI>[Harad]<INFO>ba_maison,238,44,0,101,0</INFO></NAVI> proximo to me, e entrar o aguum garden.";
			close2;
			setquest 16433;
			end;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;
			mes "[Seihyu]";
			mes "Due to o recent events of intruders, o number of dolls tem sido greatly reduced, nosso gardeners nao pode handle o tricky plant as of agora.";
			next;
			mes "[Seihyu]";
			mes "voce pode ir parum dentro o aguum garden to cacar down e secure me um ^0000CDGre Papilla^000000?";
			close3;
		case 2:
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;
			mes "[Harad]";
			mes "Voce tem secured um Gre Papilla.";
			mes "Eu tenho jum confirmed com one of o gardener dentro.";
			next;
			mes "[Harad]";
			mes "Obrigado fou ajudando out o gardeners.";
			mes "Eu vou ask fou seu ajudar novamente proximo tempo, se you have o tempo.";
			next;
			mes "[Harad]";
			mes "Eu vou dar you alguns Barmeal Tickets in retornar fou seu ajudar. Voce pode usar it freely at o mansion's restaurant.";
			close2;
			cutin "",255;
			setquest 16434;
			erasequest 16433;
			getitem 1000103,4;
			getexp 8000000,5000000;
			end;
		}
		break;
	case 3:
		switch( checkquest(16436,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			cutin "ep172_beta",2;
			mes "[Seihyu]";
			mes "Voce precisum to rest um pequeno. Oh, nao apenas you, mas o aguum garden tambem needs to rest.";
			mes "Nos temos um plant culture definir by o gardeners, entao that's it fou hoje.";

			mes "[Seihyu]";
			mes "Pou favou pegar um rest e volte depois dawn.";
			mes "Eu vou be feliz to ask fou seu ajudar novamente.";
			close3;
		case 2:
			erasequest 16436;
			break;
		}
		if (isbegin_quest(16435) == 1) {
			if (countitem(1000099) > 0) {
				cutin "ep172_beta",2;
				mes "[Seihyu]";
				mes "Voce jum checked? Como estao o gardeners?";

		cutin "ep172_beta",2;
		mes "[Seihyu]";
		mes "Due to o recent event of intruders e plant changes, gardeners are frequently replaced. Porem, hum gardeners who entered aguum garden e lost connectivity.";

		if (select( "Eu vou ir e verificar fou you.", "Refuse." ) == 2)

		cutin "ep172_beta",2;

		end;
	case 4:
		break;
	}
	cutin "ep172_beta",2;
	mes "[Seihyu]";
	mes "Voce have um moment? E sempre bom to have um ajudando mao. Eu vou be aqui. Se you have o tempo, pou favou vir e conversar to me.";
	close3;

OnInit:
	// Story
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(16428) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16429,HUNTING) == 2" );

	// Daily quests
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16431,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16433,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(16435) == 1 && countitem(1000099) > 1" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep172_watergarden == 5 && checkquest(16432,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(16431,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep172_watergarden == 5 && BaseLevel >= 180 && checkquest(16434,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(16433,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep172_watergarden == 5 && checkquest(16436,PLAYTIME) == -1 && isbegin_quest(16435) == 0" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16432,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16434,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16436,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	end;
}
